\title{Melanie-MASTERS (human masters) — King's Indian, Classical}
\author{Melanie (Deep\_Vision)}
\date{}
\begin{document}
\maketitle

\begin{abstract}Every move below was produced by relational analogy to named master games (human and/or top-engine -- the exact source is named per move). Each figure shows our position with Melanie's move (a) and the top retrieved analog positions (b--d) with the master move played there; the caption names the exact source and the piece-role correspondence under $\mu$ -- cross-type maps (a rook playing a queen's role) are called out, as no material/eval engine explains a move that way.\end{abstract}

\caption{\textbf{Move 7, White: \texttt{Rb1}.} (a) Our position; Melanie plays \texttt{Rb1} (\texttt{a1b1}). Panels (b)--(d) are the retrieved master positions whose correspondence most drove this move (highest QAP objective among those that transfer to it). (b) Retrieved from \texttt{lichess\_elite\_2024-09\_p0018\#1854} (ply 20) [structural distance $d$=61, QAP obj=47]: the master played \texttt{Bg5}; under $\mu$ our \emph{Ra1} plays the role of that game's \emph{Bc1} (a \textbf{cross-type} correspondence); cross-type roles: our \emph{B}c1 as their \emph{R}a1, our \emph{B}e2 as their \emph{Q}d1; transferred move \texttt{a1b1} \textbf{(endorses this move)}. (c) Retrieved from \texttt{lichess\_elite\_2024-09\_p0025\#1726} (ply 20) [structural distance $d$=55, QAP obj=36]: the master played \texttt{a4}; under $\mu$ our \emph{Ra1} plays the role of that game's \emph{Pa2} (a \textbf{cross-type} correspondence); cross-type roles: our \emph{b}c8 as their \emph{R}a1, our \emph{P}h2 as their \emph{B}c1; transferred move \texttt{a1b1} \textbf{(endorses this move)}. (d) Retrieved from \texttt{lichess\_elite\_2024-09\_p0017\#2723} (ply 21) [structural distance $d$=63, QAP obj=81]: the master played \texttt{Bg4}; under $\mu$ our \emph{Qd1} plays the role of that game's \emph{Bd2} (a \textbf{cross-type} correspondence); cross-type roles: our \emph{P}h2 as their \emph{R}a8, our \emph{R}a1 as their \emph{Q}d8; transferred move \texttt{d1a4}. Synthesis: 2 retrieved analogs transferred to \texttt{Rb1} (summed QAP objective 84, the strongest correspondence among the candidate moves); Melanie committed it via the \texttt{search} selector.}
\label{fig:thought-m12}
\end{figure}
